- `f:qlgggh` The scaffold's root holds `.github/workflows/check.yml`, `.dev.vars.example`, `cairn-audit.config.json`, `CLAUDE.md`, `LICENSE`, and `.claude/` (with `skills`, `agents`, and `cairn`), beside the plain files `.gitattributes`, `.gitignore`, `.prettierignore`, `.prettierrc`, `package.json`, `README.md`, and `tsconfig.json`, the configs `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, and `wrangler.jsonc`, the generated `worker-configuration.d.ts`, and the `migrations/`, `migrations-app/`, `scripts/`, and `src/` directories; under `src`, it also holds `access.ts` (the `defineAccess` map that `hooks.server.ts` hands the guard), `admin.css`, `lib/log.ts`, `content/.cairn/site-facts.json`, and `theme/theme-names.ts`. Source: `templates/waymark/` directory listing, `templates/waymark/src/access.ts:1-25`. [verified]
- `f:qnf469` `src/theme/` holds `cairn.config.ts` (one `defineAdapter` call over `content`, `backend`, `email`, `rendering`, and an `editor` group carrying `navLayout`), `icons.ts` (the `IconSet` the components and picker draw from), `markdown-components.ts` (nine `defineComponent` declarations, one hydrated), `site-config.ts` (the one `parseSiteConfig` call site, over `site.config.yaml`), `site.config.yaml` (`siteName`, `description`, primary and footer menus, and the tag `vocabulary`), `theme.css` and `site.css`, `theme-names.ts`, `components/` (`ArticleView.svelte`, `EntryRow.svelte`, `SiteFooter.svelte`, `SiteHeader.svelte`, and `admin-link.ts`), and `islands/` (`Banner.svelte`, its `registry.ts`, and `banner-expiry.ts`), where the island registry maps `banner` to `Banner.svelte`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/theme/`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/cairn.config.ts:63,177-199`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/islands/registry.ts:9`. [verified]
- `f:qx2fnd` The hidden `.cairn/` directory holds files the build writes, and a person never edits them by hand: the content manifest `index.json` and the media asset registry `media.json`. Source: `src/lib/content/compose.ts:12-13,55`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-ingest.ts:240-247`, `src/lib/vite/internal.ts:46-51`. [rejected: the build regenerates `index.json` and `site-facts.json`, but `media.json` is written by the admin's media upload, metadata, and delete commits, and a publish commit patches `index.json`; the build fails when `index.json` drifts from the markdown on disk, which is why a hand edit is unwelcome, but the build is not the only writer]
- `f:qtvesq` The scaffold's `media/[...path]/+server.ts` exports `GET = createMediaRoute({ runtime })`, an engine `RequestHandler` on `/sveltekit` that validates a 16-character hex content-hash prefix and an allow-listed image extension, then streams the object from the `MEDIA_BUCKET` binding at the content-addressed key `media/<first two hex>/<hash>.<ext>` with its own security headers, since the route sits outside `/admin`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/routes/media/[...path]/+server.ts:5-8`, `src/lib/sveltekit/media-route.ts:1-32`, `src/lib/media/naming.ts:109-113`. [verified]
- `f:16cx9j` `ContentRoutesConfig.runtime` and `NavRoutesConfig.runtime` are the composed `CairnRuntime` that the public `createContentRoutes` and `createNavRoutes` factories close over, so a developer who mounts either factory by hand, outside `createCairnAdmin`, passes it as `config.runtime`; `createCairnAdmin` forwards its own runtime to both, so a single-mount site sets neither. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts:190-192`, `src/lib/sveltekit/nav-routes.ts:46-48`, `src/lib/sveltekit/index.ts:25,56`, `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:111,120`. [verified]
- `f:qtm9y2` The scaffold's `src/routes/` holds `admin/` (`[...path]/`, `signups/`, `+layout.server.ts`, `+layout.svelte`), `(site)/` (`[...path]/`, `[...path=md]/`, `archive/[page]/`, `preview/[token]/`, `styleguide/`, `+layout.svelte`, `+page.server.ts`, `+page.svelte`), `feed.json/`, `feed.xml/`, `healthz/`, `media/[...path]/`, `robots.txt/`, `sitemap.xml/`, and the root `+error.svelte`, `+layout.server.ts`, and `+layout.svelte`; `src/params.ts` defines the `md` matcher through SvelteKit's `defineParams`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/routes/`, `templates/waymark/src/params.ts`. [verified]
- `f:1stlyv` A vitest run over code that imports `@glw907/cairn-cms` server modules sets `test.server.deps.inline: ['@glw907/cairn-cms']` when the engine installs into `node_modules`, since the engine imports `cloudflare:workers` and Node's loader rejects that scheme when vitest leaves the engine external; inlined, vitest transforms the engine and a test's `vi.mock('cloudflare:workers')` reaches its own import. The scaffold's `vitest.config.ts` carries the setting. Source: `templates/waymark/vitest.config.ts:15-20`, `examples/showcase/vitest.config.ts:11-18`. [verified]

## docs/extend/wire-the-delivery-surface.md

- `f:yef4lo` Every delivery route reads through one typed index built via `createSiteIndexes(cairn, siteConfig, { <concept>: <rawGlob> })`; Vite needs a literal glob pattern at the call site, so this cannot be looped over the concept list programmatically (one glob per concept, by name). Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-indexes.ts:38-58` (`createSiteIndexes(adapter, config, globs, opts)`, per-concept glob throw, already `f:pdgkex`/`f:4mz4tp`). [verified]
- `f:niuzzg` Every delivery route reads the site origin from one shared constant, and `ORIGIN` should be a literal, not an environment-variable read at build or request time, since a visual-regression suite needs the same origin on every run in CI and locally; a multi-environment site should give each environment its own committed config rather than branching `ORIGIN` on an env var. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:25` (`PublicRoutesConfig.origin` is a plain `string`, imposing no literal-versus-env-read constraint itself, so this is site-authoring guidance, not an engine-enforced rule), `templates/waymark/src/chassis/content.ts:35-52` (the scaffold keeps `ORIGIN` as a literal and composes `siteMeta` once for the feed, the sitemap, and the robots route). [verified: the config field and the scaffold's literal trace to source; the visual-regression rationale is an authoring recommendation, not an engine invariant]
- `f:pkt8i6` `createPublicRoutes({ site, render, origin, siteName, description })` returns `entryLoad` (resolves one entry by request path, throws 404 on a miss) and `entries` (enumerates every routable entry for the prerenderer). Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:201,214,263,270-274` `export function createPublicRoutes(config: PublicRoutesConfig): PublicRoutes` returning `{ entryLoad, entries, markdownEntries, markdownLoad }`. [verified]
- `f:03lbjg` The same factory's `markdownEntries`/`markdownLoad` build a raw-markdown twin of every routable, non-`noindex` entry at a `.md`-suffixed URL serving the entry's stored body unrendered; matched via a SvelteKit param matcher (`[...path=md]`) that claims only segments ending in `.md`, resolved ahead of the plain `[...path]` catch-all since SvelteKit prefers the more specific matched route. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:236,256` `markdownEntries`/`markdownLoad` and `templates/waymark/src/params.ts` (`md: (param) => (param.endsWith('.md') ? param : undefined)` inside `defineParams`, comment: "so `(site)/[...path=md]` claims only the raw-markdown twin path and the existing `(site)/[...path]` catch-all keeps every other route"); the "SvelteKit prefers the more specific matched route" half is framework routing precedence, not cairn source. [verified]
- `f:gwpffe` `markdownResponse` sets `Content-Type: text/markdown; charset=utf-8` deliberately, but `@sveltejs/adapter-cloudflare` (measured on 7.2.9; 8.0.0's `index.js` read the same way) does not capture a prerendered route's Response headers into the build's `_headers` file; on Cloudflare's static-asset layer (via `wrangler dev`) the header nonetheless arrives correctly, apparently because Cloudflare re-derives the content type from the `.md` extension rather than because the header survived; `vite preview` instead reports `text/markdown` with no charset at all (and `vite preview` cannot serve adapter 8 output, so that observation does not carry to it). Measured locally under `wrangler dev`, not verified against a live deployed zone, and not checked for any other file extension. Source: `src/lib/delivery/responses.ts:50-52` (`markdownResponse` sets the header); adapter-cloudflare 7.2.9's `index.js`, read 2026-09-29: it copies a project-root `_headers` file if present and appends a fixed autogenerated immutable-assets block, with no path from a prerendered route's Response headers into that file; re-measured 2026-09-29: `wrangler dev` 4.137.0 serving a static `.md` asset with no `_headers` file at all returns `Content-Type: text/markdown; charset=utf-8`, and `vite preview` (vite 8.3.0) serves the same file as `Content-Type: text/markdown` with no charset. [verified: the header, the adapter behavior, and both server responses were each re-observed on 2026-09-29; a live deployed zone and other extensions were not checked]
- `f:0elmiu` `robots.txt` should always set `disallow: ['/admin']`, since nothing about the admin route itself blocks a crawler and a site that skips this line lets a crawler index its own sign-in page. Source: `templates/waymark/src/routes/robots.txt/+server.ts:15-19` `return robotsResponse({ sitemapUrl: siteMeta.origin + '/sitemap.xml', disallow: ['/admin'], posture: (cairn as CairnAdapter).aiPosture });` (the scaffold's own robots route sets exactly this). [verified]
- `f:xafwcw` `robotsResponse` takes a `posture` option for declaring an AI-training-crawler stance, covered separately in "Choose an AI posture". Source: `src/lib/delivery/robots.ts` (`posture` option, already `f:rh0xv5`). [verified]
- `f:hv8qs9` `createSiteIndexes(adapter, config, globs)` returns one typed index per declared concept, keyed by concept id, plus `site`, the cross-concept `SiteResolver`; `site` is a reserved key, so a concept cannot be named `site`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-indexes.ts#createSiteIndexes`. [verified]
- `f:pvk6h5` Each delivery surface has a pure builder and a `Response` wrapper, all exported from `@glw907/cairn-cms/delivery`: `buildRssFeed` and `rssResponse(channel, items)`, `buildJsonFeed` and `jsonFeedResponse`, `buildSitemap` and `sitemapResponse(urls)` (served as `application/xml; charset=utf-8`), `buildRobots` and `robotsResponse`, plus `markdownResponse`. A `FeedChannel` is `{ title, description, siteUrl, feedUrl }` with optional `language` and `author`; a `FeedItem` needs `title`, `url`, and `summary` with optional `date`, `updated`, `contentHtml`, and `tags`; a `SitemapUrl` is `{ loc, lastmod? }`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/responses.ts#sitemapResponse`, `src/lib/delivery/feeds.ts#FeedChannel`, `src/lib/delivery/feeds.ts#FeedItem`, `src/lib/delivery/sitemap.ts#SitemapUrl`, `src/lib/delivery/data.ts:54-72`. [verified]
- `f:d7v5cg` `createLinkResolver(site)` returns a `LinkResolve` that maps a `cairn:` reference to its permalink and throws when the target is missing or belongs to a non-routable concept, so a feed that renders bodies through it fails the build on a dangling link. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-resolver.ts#createLinkResolver`. [verified]
- `f:8k9xwm` The scaffold's `sitemap.xml`, `feed.xml`, and `robots.txt` routes each export `prerender = true`, so a build emits `/sitemap.xml`, `/feed.xml`, and `/robots.txt` as static files that `npm run preview` then serves. Source: `templates/waymark/src/routes/sitemap.xml/+server.ts:5`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/feed.xml/+server.ts:6`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/robots.txt/+server.ts:7`. [verified]
- `f:py57s1` Adapter-cloudflare 8's worker caches no response in `caches.default`, where 7.2.9's put every response carrying a public `Cache-Control` into it through worktop's `cfw.cache`, `/media` and public SSR pages included; `/media` answers `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable`, so browsers still hold the bytes, and the engine adds no cache of its own, since a Cache API purge reaches one data center. A site that relied on the edge copy adds its own caching. Source: `@sveltejs/adapter-cloudflare` 8.0.0 `files/worker.js` (no `caches` use) and 7.2.9 `files/worker.js` (`caches.default`), read 2026-10-06; `src/lib/sveltekit/media-route.ts:38`. [external: SvelteKit adapter-cloudflare]

## Code sweep (2026-09-30)

Facts a code-first gap sweep found missing from the container, each re-read at HEAD by an
independent verifier before filing. Grouped by the extend page they feed, in the outline's group
order; the record is `docs/superpowers/research/2026-09-30-extend-gap-sweep.md`.

### architecture

- `f:025q6u` The `Backend` interface contract: `commit` with `expectedHead` makes one attempt and throws `CommitConflictError` on a head mismatch, while omitting `expectedHead` keeps the head-merge retry; `readFile` returns null for a missing path; `listCommits` returns an empty array rather than throwing for a missing file; `createBranch` returns the sha it branched at and throws `BranchExistsError` on a name collision; `deleteBranch` treats a missing branch as success. Source: `src/lib/github/backend.ts#Backend`. [verified]
- `f:0gihxq` Entry save, single publish, publish-all, entry delete and rename, and the media delete and metadata commits use the head-merge retry (three retries after the first attempt, then a conflict), while the nav, tidy-settings, vocabulary, media upload manifest, and revert commits are head-guarded and fail on the first stale head. Source: `src/lib/github/repo.ts:194-304`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-revert.ts:153-158`. [verified]
- `f:0oyrh6` Publish-all commits every pending entry the editor can reach, plus the content manifest, as one commit to the default branch titled 'Publish N entries' (or 'Publish 1 entry'), so one deploy fires. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-write.ts:476-486`. [verified]
- `f:0xxou5` After a publish lands, the engine deletes the entry's pending branch only when the branch head still equals the sha the publish captured, so a save that lands during the publish keeps the entry pending. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-write.ts:401-405,504-516`. [verified]

### add-cairn-to-a-sveltekit-app

- `f:1b54g7` A custom `auth.send` (`SendMagicLink`) receives the built `MagicLinkMessage` (`to`, `from`, `subject`, `html`, `text`, plus optional `cc`, `bcc`, `replyTo`, and `attachments`), where `replyTo` takes one address, not an array; the text of anything it throws reaches the log scrubbed of token values and truncated, so a thrown message must never embed the message body or the sign-in link. Source: `src/lib/email.ts#MagicLinkMessage`, `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts:150-154`. [verified]
- `f:2gtftn` The magic-link email's subject ('Sign in to' plus the site name) and body, including the fixed sentence 'The link expires in 10 minutes', are engine copy; the site name comes from the site config and the sender from the adapter's `email` group, a supplied `auth.branding` (`siteName`, `from`, `replyTo`) replaces all three together, and any other change goes through a custom `auth.send` that edits the `MagicLinkMessage` it receives. Source: `src/lib/email.ts#buildMagicLinkMessage`, `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:103-109`. [verified]
- `f:g81luc` `AuthRoutesConfig.bootstrapOwner` is an optional `{ email, displayName }` pair, both members required strings, that `createCairnAdmin` forwards from `auth.bootstrapOwner`: when the sign-in request action receives that email (compared trimmed and lowercased) and the `editor` table is empty, it inserts the owner row atomically before the allowlist lookup, so the first sign-in proceeds like any allow-listed editor's; a non-matching email or a non-empty table grants nothing, which replaces a hand-run `wrangler d1 execute` INSERT. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts#AuthRoutesConfig.bootstrapOwner`, `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts:196-203`, `src/lib/auth/store.ts:460-475`, `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:110`. [verified]
- `f:v72g9z` `AuthBranding` has three members, `siteName` and `from` (required strings) and `replyTo` (an optional single address), and `AuthRoutesConfig.branding` is required on `createAuthRoutes` but optional through `createCairnAdmin`'s `auth`, a `Partial<AuthRoutesConfig>`: absent, it defaults to `runtime.siteName`, `runtime.sender.from`, and `runtime.sender.replyTo`; supplied, it replaces the default whole, so a `branding` that leaves `replyTo` off sends with no reply-to even when the adapter's `email` group sets one. `AuthRoutesConfig.send` is the optional `SendMagicLink` that replaces `cloudflareSend`. Source: `src/lib/email.ts#AuthBranding`, `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts#AuthRoutesConfig`, `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts:164`, `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:41,103-110`. [verified]
- `f:ebx4pv` `SiteConfig.siteName` is the one required key, a non-empty string that `composeRuntime` carries as `runtime.siteName`, the name the admin shell shows and the sign-in email's subject and default branding use; `description`, `author`, and `locale` are optional strings `parseSiteConfig` accepts that no `src/lib` module reads, left for the site's own code (the scaffold's content module reads `siteConfig.description` as its site description). Source: `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts#SiteConfig`, `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts:321-329`, `src/lib/content/compose.ts:38`, `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:106`, `templates/waymark/src/chassis/content.ts:51`. [verified]

### scaffolded-site-files

- `f:2qnqkm` A scaffolded site serves a themed 404 through two pieces: a root `src/routes/+error.svelte` that rebuilds the site chrome itself (the prerendered `(site)` group's catch-all is stripped from the runtime manifest, so that group's layout never runs for an unmatched path), and `assets.not_found_handling` set to `"none"` in `wrangler.jsonc`, which passes an unmatched request to the Worker; `"404-page"` would serve an unthemed static file from the edge without invoking the Worker. Source: `templates/waymark/wrangler.jsonc:11-20`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/+error.svelte:1-11`. [verified]
- `f:2s8u70` In the scaffold, `/admin/nav` edits only `menus.primary`; the `menus.footer` block is a flat menu the developer edits directly in `site.config.yaml`, read through `readMenu(siteConfig, 'footer', 1)`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/theme/site-config.ts:25-30`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/cairn.config.ts:181-186`. [verified]
- `f:2zl3qz` The scaffold's `handleUnseenRoutes` exempts only `/(site)/archive/[page]`, because a corpus that fits on page one gives that route no entries and SvelteKit's crawl-completeness check would otherwise fail the build; any other unseen prerenderable route still throws. Source: `templates/waymark/svelte.config.js:37-48`. [verified]
- `f:34rsss` A link to `/admin` in prerendered public markup carries `rel="external"` so SvelteKit's crawler never requests it, because every `/admin` route answers a build-time crawl with an error and the scaffold's `handleHttpError` throws on any HTTP error; the scaffold's header and footer apply it through the shared `isAdminHref` predicate. Source: `templates/waymark/src/theme/components/admin-link.ts:1-10`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/components/SiteHeader.svelte:110`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/components/SiteFooter.svelte:68`, `templates/waymark/svelte.config.js:26-36`. [verified]
- `f:38pjqy` `ARCHIVE_PAGE_SIZE` in `src/chassis/archive.ts` (13 in the scaffold) is the one page-size knob for both the home page and `/archive/[page]`; both paginate every post after the newest one, which the home shows as its lead, so `/archive/2` exists only once more than 14 posts are published. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/archive.ts:10`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/(site)/+page.server.ts:17-18`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/(site)/archive/[page]/+page.server.ts:13`. [verified]
- `f:3bbeia` The setup command's Workers Builds chapter registers the Cloudflare API token the admin pasted as the build token named `cairn create-cairn-site build token` (reusing an existing build token that wraps the same token id), so revoking or rolling that token at Cloudflare breaks automatic deploys with no prior warning: the next commit still triggers a build, and the build fails. Source: `packages/create-cairn-site/src/cloudflare/chapter3.mjs:94,1068-1079`, `packages/create-cairn-site/README.md:322-330`. [verified]
- `f:3m0oxs` The setup command saves its progress, including the pasted Cloudflare token, in `~/.config/cairn/sites/<id>.json` (directory mode 0700, file mode 0600), never under the project directory; a `--yes` run reads the token from `CAIRN_CF_API_TOKEN` and refuses a token-shaped command-line value. Source: `packages/create-cairn-site/src/state.mjs:24-27,57-61`, `packages/create-cairn-site/src/cloudflare/prefill.mjs:231-273`. [verified]
- `f:3my5c1` On a resumed setup run, `--org`, `--repo-name`, `--app-name`, and `--owner-email` override the saved answer for any chapter that has not yet run. Source: `packages/create-cairn-site/bin.mjs:583-595`, `packages/create-cairn-site/src/github/chapter.mjs:154-164`. [verified]
- `f:498l97` `--brand-color` accepts a `#`-prefixed hex color, an `oklch(...)` string, or a bare number read as a hue in degrees (the prompt asks for 0 to 360, a range the flag does not enforce), and any other shape fails. Source: `packages/create-cairn-site/src/substitute.mjs:158-168`, `packages/create-cairn-site/src/prompts.mjs:16`. [verified]
- `f:4ax489` `cairn-guidance check` reports seven lines and exits 0 by default; `--strict` exits 1 only when the guidance tree itself is stale or missing. Source: `src/lib/guidance/bin.ts:107-111`, `src/lib/guidance/check.ts:223-236`. [verified]
- `f:4h9fz4` `cairn-guidance install` never deletes: a path an earlier `MANIFEST` listed that the package no longer ships is printed as removable and left for the site to remove. Source: `src/lib/guidance/install.ts:229,395`, `src/lib/guidance/bin.ts:100`. [verified]
- `f:4nccq6` `cairn-guidance install` never writes `.claude/settings.json`, so the `Stop` hook that runs `npm run check:cairn --if-present` is a snippet, `claude/snippets/settings-hook.json`, that the developer pastes in deliberately. Source: `claude/snippets/settings-hook.json:1-15`, `docs/reference/guidance.md:32-34,146`. [verified]
- `f:5ohm23` `cairn-guidance install` exits 0 even when an entry is refused or a write fails (each printed to stderr), so a script that must detect a partial install reads stderr rather than the exit code. Source: `src/lib/guidance/bin.ts:86-104`. [verified]
- `f:666eg6` The scaffold's `predev`, `prebuild`, and `precheck` scripts compile the gitignored `.cairn/admin.css`, so a Workers Builds `npm run build` needs no separate admin-stylesheet step. Source: `templates/waymark/package.json:7,10,15`, `templates/waymark/.gitignore:5`. [verified]
- `f:690k0p` The scaffold's `package.json` also ships `cairn:manifest` (runs `cairn-manifest`), `format` and `format:check` (Prettier), and `test:unit` (`vitest run`). Source: `templates/waymark/package.json:12,18-20`. [verified]

### content-model

- `f:69j91g` The engine reads the frontmatter keys `title` (falling back to the id), `date`, `updated` (the sitemap `lastmod` and the feed's modified date), `description` (the excerpt), and `draft` by literal name, so a site that wants one to survive an admin save declares it as a field; a boolean field named `draft` is also what the edit page renders as its Hidden toggle. Source: `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:106,126-128`, `src/lib/content/manifest.ts:104-110`, `src/lib/delivery/views.ts:32`, `src/lib/admin/EditPage.svelte:1398-1402`. [verified]
- `f:7ak73t` A concept permalink must start with `/` and may use only the tokens `:slug`, `:year`, `:month`, and `:day`; a date token throws unless the concept's routing is `feed`, and `:month` and `:day` render zero-padded. Source: `src/lib/content/concepts.ts:16-19,101-119`, `src/lib/content/url-policy.ts:35-53`. [verified]
- `f:7ci1fm` A `url` field accepts only an absolute `http://` or `https://` URL with no whitespace, so a root-relative path or a `mailto:` value fails validation. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:14-15,275-277`. [verified]
- `f:7l7y5l` A text or textarea `pattern` is compiled as `new RegExp(source)` with no flags and tested unanchored, so a pattern carries its own `^` and `$` to constrain the whole value. Source: `src/lib/content/field-rules.ts:6-11,28-31`. [verified]
- `f:7lo8fe` A field `default` only prefills a new entry's create form (a date field's `'today'` resolves to the current UTC date, and an empty-string or `false` default is omitted); it is never applied to an existing or hand-written file. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:505-524`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-read.ts:326`. [verified]
- `f:7m8nd4` Entry ids are lowercase ASCII letters, digits, and single hyphens, so `slugify` turns a title in a non-Latin script into an empty address (the create action then refuses it) and treats an accented letter as a separator rather than transliterating it (`résumé` becomes `r-sum`). Source: `src/lib/content/ids.ts:5-11,32-38`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-read.ts:233-235`. [verified]
- `f:8b21y6` A `multiselect` that sets `options` rejects any value outside them even with `creatable: true`, which only changes the control to a tag input. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:209-222`. [verified]
- `f:8igohk` A `datetime` field does not enforce its `min` or `max`, keeps a quoted string value as written, and converts a value YAML parsed into a `Date` (an unquoted full ISO timestamp) to a zoneless `YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm` string read in UTC, dropping seconds and offset. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:116-122,298-300`, `src/lib/content/frontmatter.ts:217-239`. [verified]
- `f:8zdux6` `ConceptConfig.dir` is used verbatim in `${dir}/${id}.md` paths and is not validated, so it stays repo-relative with no leading or trailing slash, matching the concept's content glob; entries sit directly in `dir`, never in subfolders. Source: `src/lib/content/cross-branch-index.ts:98`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-list.ts:103`, `src/lib/media/rewrite-plan.ts:104`, `src/lib/github/backend.ts:32`. [verified]
- `f:9270mo` The edit page raises a warn-only `address-collision` advisory when another published entry on main resolves to the same permalink; publishing re-checks main plus every open `cairn/*` branch and, when a collision remains, logs `publish.address_collided` after the publish lands; neither check blocks a save or a publish, and both fail open. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-read.ts:394-419`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-write.ts:356-391`, `src/lib/content/advisories.ts:65-119`. [verified]

### define-an-adapter-and-schema

- `f:93iwom` In `defineFieldset(record, { refine, behavior })`, `behavior[field].validate(value, siblings)` runs only for a field that passed its own checks, receives the coerced value and the raw frontmatter, and returns an error string or null, and a throw is logged as `content.field_behavior_failed` and treated as valid; `refine(data, body)` runs synchronously only when no per-field or behavior issue exists and returns a field-keyed error map, or nothing, that fails the save; a behavior key naming an undeclared field throws at declaration. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts#defineFieldset`. [verified]
- `f:97c6g1` On an `array` field of objects, `itemLabel` names the sub-field whose value summarizes each collapsed row in the editor; an empty value falls back to the field label plus the row number, and an array of leaf items summarizes a row by its own value. Source: `src/lib/admin/RepeatableField.svelte:119-148`. [verified]
- `f:9ro4u5` `summaryFields` on a concept names frontmatter keys the content index copies, from the validated frontmatter, onto each `ContentSummary.fields` record; a key the concept's fieldset does not declare throws at composition, and omitting `summaryFields` leaves `fields` empty. Source: `src/lib/content/concepts.ts:145-152`, `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:118-121`. [verified]
- `f:9sba8e` `parseSiteConfig` rejects the adapter group keys `backend`, `email`, `rendering`, `media`, and `editor` with a message naming `cairn.config.ts` as their home, rejects `content` with a message to move `permalink` and `datePrefix` into `defineConcept`, and rejects any other unknown top-level key by listing the known ones. Source: `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts#parseSiteConfig`. [verified]
- `f:a81e6y` US English is the only spellcheck dictionary cairn ships, so a `spellcheck.dialect` set to anything else, or left unset, silently resolves to it. Source: `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts#dictionaryFileForDialect`. [verified]
- `f:adsn4l` Setting `editor.supportContact` to an explicit empty string, unlike leaving it unset, passes through `composeRuntime` untouched, and the Help home then renders no hand-off. Source: `src/lib/content/compose.ts:44`, `src/lib/admin/HelpHome.svelte:82-83,285-289`. [verified]
- `f:aj5c4z` A failed admin save shows one banner carrying only the first message of the validator's errors map (the first failing field in declaration order, or the first `refine` message when every field passes); the `issues` paths are not routed to inputs, so a `refine` message reads as a standalone sentence. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-write.ts:116-118,181-184`, `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:473-487`, `src/lib/admin/EditPage.svelte:1030`. [verified]
- `f:apoa3k` The validator's normalized frontmatter trims strings and omits empty optional fields, unchecked booleans (never `false`), and empty arrays and objects, so a site reads an absent key as unset or false, and `InferFieldset` types every non-required field as optional. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:100-110,163-166,200-203,210-213,262-266`. [verified]
- `f:ar5p72` A fieldset `refine` function that throws is not caught: the throw fails the admin save with a server error and aborts the build's content index, unlike `behavior.validate`, whose throw is logged and treated as valid. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:460-471,485`, `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:103-111`. [verified]

### link-content-with-references

- `f:aulk5e` A `cairn:` token is resolved, counted in the manifest's link graph, and build-checked only as an inline markdown link in an entry body (or in a fragment spliced into one); in a raw HTML anchor or a reference-style link definition it survives the sanitize floor as an inert literal `cairn:` href, in a component attribute or frontmatter it is never rewritten, and none of these fail the build. Source: `src/lib/render/resolve-links.ts:19-34`, `src/lib/content/links.ts:52-64`, `src/lib/render/sanitize-schema.ts:43-60`. [verified]
- `f:bf47a8` `createLinkResolver` resolves a `cairn:` link to a draft entry to that entry's permalink instead of throwing, so the build succeeds and the published link points at a 404 until the target is published. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-resolver.ts:190-196`, `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:153`. [verified]

### reuse-content-across-entries

- `f:bfiuem` An `::include` with no `fragment` attribute always renders a calm notice and logs `include.missing` with `reason: 'empty_fragment'`, even in the public build, because the resolver is never called; a named fragment the resolver returns nothing for renders a `Missing fragment` notice and logs `reason: 'not_found'`, while a throwing resolver, which the public build supplies, fails the build instead. Source: `src/lib/render/resolve-include.ts:126-151`. [verified]

### configure-rendering

- `f:bnqj5z` A component's repeatable slot may declare `itemLabel(item, index)` to name each row in the insert form; when it returns nothing or blank text, the row reads as the slot label plus the row number. Source: `src/lib/render/registry.ts#SlotDef`, `src/lib/admin/ComponentForm.svelte:108-114`. [verified]
- `f:c7ruij` A `ComponentDef` shapes its picker row with `icon` (an IconSet glyph key), `group` (its heading), `hidden` (left out of the top-level picker), `insertTemplate` (the scaffold inserted at the cursor), `use` (one-line guidance), and `preview` (a sample whose presence opts the configure step into the two-pane live preview); a non-empty `defaultIconByRole` overrides the engine's role-to-glyph defaults and makes `defineRegistry` throw when the component declares no `type: 'icon'` attribute. Source: `src/lib/render/registry.ts#ComponentDef`, `src/lib/render/registry.ts:172-176`. [verified]
- `f:cefzv5` A component's `slots` declare `kind` as `'inline'`, `'markdown'`, or `'repeatable'` (with `itemFields` and an optional `itemLabel`); `build(ctx)` reads `ctx.attributes`, `ctx.slot(name)` (the children, an empty array when absent), `ctx.items(name)` (one child list per repeatable item), `ctx.attr(key)` (the string value, undefined for a boolean or absent attribute), and `ctx.node`, and the engine stamps the entrance ordinal on the top-level result so `build` stays free of motion. Source: `src/lib/render/registry.ts#ComponentContext`, `src/lib/render/rehype-dispatch.ts:165-168`. [verified]
- `f:cgwyp6` `defineRegistry` throws for a component named `figure` or `include`, the directive names the engine's own render step handles, with a message telling the developer to remove the component if the built-in covers the use or to rename it. Source: `src/lib/render/registry.ts#defineRegistry`, `src/lib/render/registry.ts:159-170`. [verified]
- `f:co54dg` A component with named slots is written with a four-colon outer fence and each named slot as a nested `:::slotName` container, a repeatable slot's items are bullet lines inside its container, and a directive's bracketed label is dropped when the component declares no `title` slot. Source: `src/lib/render/component-grammar.ts:45-72,117-150`, `src/lib/render/remark-directives.ts:91-110`. [verified]
- `f:dat0dd` The scaffold wires `proseTypography` (remark-smartypants with `dashes: 'oldschool'`) into its one `createRenderer(registry, { remarkPlugins })` call, so straight quotes, `--` and `---`, and `...` in body prose are smartened identically in the public render and the editor preview, while inline code, code blocks, and link URLs stay untouched. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/render.ts:40-50`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/cairn.config.ts:33-37`. [verified]
- `f:db26u3` The render pipeline's highlighter passes a fenced `mermaid` block through untouched, keeping `<code class="language-mermaid">` for a site's client-side diagram renderer, since Shiki has no mermaid grammar. Source: `src/lib/render/highlight.ts:60-64`. [verified]
- `f:dni13r` `rehypeCairnHighlight` preloads js, ts, jsx, tsx, svelte, html, css, json, bash, markdown, python, yaml, and sql with their aliases; a fence in any other language, or with none, renders as plain `pre.shiki` text with no token classes and never throws, and only `mermaid` passes through untouched. Source: `src/lib/render/highlight.ts:36-64,242-248`. [verified]
- `f:dw4rbb` `rehypeTableScroll` wraps each rendered table in `div.table-scroll` with `role="region"`, `tabindex="0"`, and an `aria-label` of `Table: ` plus the first header row's `th` text comma-joined (or `Table` when there is none), and keeps the table a real table inside. Source: `src/lib/render/table-scroll.ts:14-39,50-62`. [verified]
- `f:e11npv` The engine's `:::figure` directive keeps a placement class only when its `class` is exactly `center`, `wide`, or `full` (emitted as `cairn-place-<role>`), silently ignores any other class, and renders a figure with no `media:` image as a `<figure>` around its remaining children without throwing. Source: `src/lib/render/remark-figure.ts:19-20,76-92`. [verified]

### configure-media

- `f:enuyhg` `AssetConfig.allowedTypes` filters the server-sniffed type of an upload and can only narrow what the engine stores (JPEG, PNG, GIF, WebP, AVIF): a sniffed HEIC or unrecognized type is refused as `unsupported_type` whatever `allowedTypes` lists, and so is any upload declared as `image/svg+xml`, `image/svg`, `text/html`, or `application/xml`, or whose first non-whitespace byte is `<`. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-ingest.ts:132-139`, `src/lib/media/sniff.ts:27,33,83,118-129`. [verified]
- `f:ett331` The upload action refuses a request lacking a positive integer `Content-Length` as `length_required` (a `fail(411)`) and a declared or actual size above `maxUploadBytes` as `too_large` (a `fail(413)`); the header is client-advisory, so the effective ceiling is the lower of `maxUploadBytes` and the Worker request-body limit. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-ingest.ts:96-103,126-130`. [verified]
- `f:ex4hss` `AssetConfig.publicBase` (default `/media`) sets the URL prefix the engine writes into rendered images, the editor, and the Library, but it does not move the delivery route: the site mounts `createMediaRoute` at `<publicBase>/[...path]` with the catch-all param named `path`, or every generated image URL 404s. Source: `src/lib/media/naming.ts:123-141`, `src/lib/sveltekit/media-route.ts:94-103`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/media/[...path]/+server.ts`. [verified]
- `f:f4ulxz` The media route reads only the last path segment's hash and extension, so the slug and any fan-out directory in the URL are ignored, and the slug and opaque URL forms resolve to the same object. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/media-route.ts:112-125`. [verified]
- `f:fjnszk` The media route sets `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Content-Disposition: inline`, `Content-Security-Policy: default-src 'none'; sandbox`, and `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` on every non-404 response, and serves an object stored without a content type as `application/octet-stream`. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/media-route.ts:28-42`. [verified]
- `f:frgg7d` On a media-enabled site using `createMediaResolver`, a `media:` image whose hash has no manifest row logs `media.resolve_missing` and renders as a broken image (`src="#"`, the class `cairn-broken-media`, the title `Missing media asset`, its alt kept), and the public build still succeeds, unlike a dangling `cairn:` link, which fails the prerender; a site that wants a failed build wraps the resolver to throw on `undefined`. Source: `src/lib/render/resolve-media.ts:89-101,175-198`, `src/lib/render/resolve-shared.ts:16-23`. [verified]
- `f:fxit31` Media usage tracking counts a `media:` reference only in a top-level `image` field's `src` and in body markdown image nodes, so a token in a text field, a link, a directive or component attribute, or raw HTML is not counted, and the Library can report no references for an asset a page still renders. Source: `src/lib/content/media-refs.ts:30-60`, `src/lib/media/usage.ts:18-21`. [verified]
- `f:g4cwcw` The upload action takes the raw file bytes as a `text/plain` body with `Content-Length`, the `X-Cairn-CSRF` header, and optional URI-encoded `X-Cairn-Filename`, `X-Cairn-Alt` (capped at 160 characters), `X-Cairn-Display-Name`, `X-Cairn-Width`, and `X-Cairn-Height` headers; it refuses bytes the sniff does not place in the allowed types as `unsupported_type` whatever the declared type, and every refusal arrives as a `fail()` inside the 200 JSON action envelope, not as an HTTP status. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-ingest.ts:96-150`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-shared.ts:23`. [verified]
- `f:9awije` Cloudflare caps a Worker's request body size by the Cloudflare account plan, not the Workers plan, and answers a larger request with a `413 Request entity too large` error, so raising `maxUploadBytes` past the plan's cap has no effect; the cap per plan is on Cloudflare's Workers limits page. Source: https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/platform/limits/#request-limits. [external: Cloudflare Workers limits]

### migrate-existing-content

- `f:ggzzur` An admin save writes the file's frontmatter from the validator's normalized output alone, so a frontmatter key the concept's fieldset does not declare is deleted from that entry the first time an editor saves it; a site declares every key it wants to keep. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-entry-write.ts:181,213`, `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:452-458`. [verified]
- `f:gh4ckg` A text, textarea, select, url, or email field reads a non-string YAML value (an unquoted number such as 1984, a date-shaped scalar, or a boolean) as empty, so a required field fails validation and an optional one is dropped on the next save; quoting the value in the file fixes it, since only number, date, and datetime fields accept a parsed number or `Date`. Source: `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:116-122,262-266`. [verified]

### theme-your-public-site

- `f:gnn3pv` The admin editor's preview frame loads none of the site's CSS: a site names its compiled stylesheets in `editor.preview.stylesheets` (resolved with Vite `?url` imports), with an optional `bodyClass` and an optional `containerClass` wrapping the rendered content, and `byConcept` overrides those two classes per concept id while the stylesheets stay shared; with no `preview` set, the frame renders unstyled markup behind a hint that says so. Source: `src/lib/content/types.ts#PreviewConfig`, `src/lib/admin/preview-doc.ts:72-116`, `src/lib/admin/EditPage.svelte:2111`. [verified]
- `f:gtg454` The scaffold references `theme.css` and `site.css` only through Vite `?url` imports, because a static import folds the sheet into a layout CSS chunk whose basename differs between the client and server builds, so the server-resolved URL the preview frame links would 404 in the browser. Source: `templates/waymark/src/theme/site.css:9-17`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/cairn.config.ts:25-29`. [verified]
- `f:guthtp` The editor preview document's root element carries `data-cairn-preview`, the hook a site's stylesheet uses to suppress entrance animations (for example on `[data-rise]`) inside the preview, and every link click in the frame is inert. Source: `src/lib/admin/preview-doc.ts#buildPreviewDoc`, `src/lib/admin/EditPage.svelte:2130`. [verified]
- `f:gzw7os` The chassis's `--spacing-3xs`, `--spacing-2xs`, `--spacing-xs`, `--spacing-xl`, and `--spacing-2xl` share suffixes with five Tailwind `--container-*` keys, and Tailwind resolves `max-w-<key>` to the spacing variable (`max-w-2xl` compiles to `max-width: var(--spacing-2xl)`, about 4rem, not 42rem), so a theme uses `max-w-measure`, `max-w-measure-wide`, or an arbitrary value instead. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/README.md:107-118`, `templates/waymark/src/chassis/tokens.css:113-120`. [verified]
- `f:h1qxlq` An unlayered rule in a site stylesheet always beats a Tailwind utility, which lives in `@layer utilities`, so a container class using the `margin: 0 auto` shorthand silently cancels `mt-*` and `mb-*` on the same element; single-axis longhands such as `margin-inline: auto` avoid it. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/README.md:120-135`. [verified]
- `f:hectgs` Waymark's `site.css` sets `html { font-size: clamp(1rem, 0.7631rem + 0.2631vw, 1.125rem) }`, growing the root from 16px to 18px between about 1440px and 2200px, so rem-based layout scales for ultrawide screens while a fixed px dimension does not. Source: `templates/waymark/src/theme/site.css:44`. [verified]
- `f:hgal3e` `formatDate` in `src/chassis/date.ts` is the one date vocabulary for the archive and article surfaces, hard-coded to the `en-GB` locale and UTC (`9 Jul 2026`), so changing the format or locale is a one-file edit. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/date.ts:4-17`. [verified]
- `f:i3rn6f` `src/app.html` carries an inline no-flash script that hard-codes the theme cookie (`cairn-site-theme`) and both daisyUI theme names, so renaming a theme means editing `theme-names.ts`, that script, and the two `@plugin "daisyui/theme"` names in `theme.css`; `theme-names.test.ts` fails when they drift. Source: `templates/waymark/src/app.html:6-18`, `templates/waymark/src/theme/theme-names.ts:1-22`. [verified]
- `f:i80vsl` Class prefixes name their owner: `cairn-*` is shared by the engine and the chassis (a theme colors it through tokens and never restyles its structure), `site-*` is the theme's own chrome, `sg-*` belongs to the styleguide route alone, and an unprefixed directive class such as `.callout` comes from the theme's `markdown-components.ts` and may be renamed. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/README.md:47-69`. [verified]
- `f:ivp8wl` `prose.css` keeps its signature flourishes (the cairn-glyph rule, the diamond bullet, the margin-hanging pull quote) behind `.prose[data-flourish]`, off by default; a theme opts in with one `data-flourish` attribute on its `.prose` root. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/README.md:136-139`, `templates/waymark/src/chassis/prose.css:35-36`. [verified]
- `f:j2qzct` `cairn-media-seed` fills wrangler's local R2 state, which the media route reads only when the dev backend is off; the scaffold's `npm run dev` sets `CAIRN_DEV_BACKEND=1`, whose handle serves `/media` from an in-memory fake bucket, so seeded objects appear under `npx vite dev` without that variable or under `wrangler dev`. Source: `packages/cairn-cms-dev/src/handle.ts:104-146`, `templates/waymark/scripts/dev.mjs:23-27`, `templates/waymark/src/hooks.server.ts:19-24`. [verified]

### build-the-public-routes

- `f:j2rndg` The public entry's SEO `description` is the frontmatter `description`, else the entry excerpt, else `PublicRoutesConfig.description`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts#composeEntryData`, `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:167`. [verified]
- `f:j81xye` `composeEntryData` types a dated entry `article` (Article JSON-LD, `article:published_time`, `article:modified_time` when `updated` is set, and the RSS and JSON Feed alternate links) and an undated one `website` (WebSite JSON-LD), and `article:author` is emitted only for a dated entry. Source: `src/lib/delivery/seo.ts#buildSeoMeta`, `src/lib/delivery/seo.ts:55-86`, `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:179`. [verified]
- `f:jguiox` The public entry's social image is the resolved hero's absolute URL, else the frontmatter `image` string, else `PublicRoutesConfig.defaultImage`, each anchored to the origin; `twitter:image:alt` is the hero's alt when non-empty, and `twitter:card` is `summary_large_image` only when an image exists. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:154-157`, `src/lib/delivery/seo.ts:42`. [verified]
- `f:jsh6ae` `readSeoFields` reads `description`, `image`, `robots`, and `author` off an entry's validated frontmatter (trimmed strings, empty omitted), so `image`, `robots`, and `author` reach the head only when the concept's fieldset declares them; an undeclared key is not on the normalized frontmatter and is ignored. Source: `src/lib/delivery/seo-fields.ts#readSeoFields`, `src/lib/content/fieldset.ts:452-456`. [verified]
- `f:k5uws5` `composeEntryData` derives `heroImage` and the social image only from the frontmatter key `image`; an image field under another name (for example `cover`) with the `seo` flag validates and renders in the editor but is not read at delivery, so a site keeps its hero field named `image`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts#deriveHeroImage`. [verified]
- `f:k7nln3` `createPublicRoutes` builds SEO metadata only for entry pages; a home, archive, or tag page has no description, canonical link, or Open Graph tags unless the site calls `buildSeoMeta` and renders `CairnHead` itself, and the scaffold's home page sets only a `<title>`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:160-180,214-217`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/(site)/+page.svelte:44-46`. [verified]
- `f:kdo96n` `SeoMeta.jsonLd` is a single schema.org `Article` object (`headline`, `description`, `url`, and when known `datePublished`, `dateModified`, `image`) for a dated entry or a `WebSite` object (`name`, `description`, `url`) for an undated one, never carries the author, and a site adds richer types by replacing `seo.jsonLd` before `CairnHead` renders it. Source: `src/lib/delivery/seo.ts:1-3,69-86`, `src/lib/delivery/CairnHead.svelte:56`. [verified]
- `f:kv3qen` `createSiteIndexes(adapter, config, globs, opts)` validates every non-draft entry's frontmatter against its concept's validator when it builds the site resolver and throws one error listing each invalid field as `<dir>/<id>: <field>: <message>`, so malformed content fails the build; `opts.validate: false` skips the check, an invalid draft never fails it, and every invalid entry, draft or not, is left out of the index and recorded in `ContentIndex.problems()`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-resolver.ts:48-72`, `src/lib/delivery/site-indexes.ts:38-67`, `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:108-111`. [verified]
- `f:ky39tz` `createSiteResolver` throws at build with `permalink "<p>" resolves to both "<a>" and "<b>"` when two non-draft entries of routable concepts share a permalink, whether or not validation is on; drafts and entries of a non-routable concept never enter that union, so they cannot collide. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-resolver.ts:77-92`. [verified]
- `f:m9z6sr` `ContentIndex.all()` and `byTag()` exclude entries whose frontmatter is `draft: true` unless called with `{ includeDrafts: true }`, `allTags()` and `adjacent()` always exclude them, and the site resolver's permalink lookup is built from `all()`, so a draft never resolves or prerenders publicly, even after publishing, while `byId` still returns it. Source: `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:148-178`, `src/lib/delivery/site-resolver.ts:84-92`. [verified]
- `f:mc3n4w` `buildSitemapView(site, descriptors, origin, extraRoutes?)` lists `extraRoutes` (root-relative paths such as `/about`, origin-anchored) first with no `lastmod`, then every non-draft entry of each routable concept with `lastmod` from `updated`, else `date`; a site page that is not a concept entry (an about page, a tag index) appears only when passed in `extraRoutes`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/views.ts#buildSitemapView`, `src/lib/delivery/views.ts:10-38`. [verified]
- `f:ms7l5u` An entry whose `robots` frontmatter carries `noindex` still appears in `buildSitemapView`'s rows, the scaffold's sitemap, and the scaffold's feeds, because only the head's robots meta tag and the raw-markdown twin read that field; a site that wants it out filters on the entry's frontmatter itself. Source: `src/lib/delivery/views.ts:19-37`, `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:225-257`, `templates/waymark/src/routes/sitemap.xml/+server.ts:8-13`, `templates/waymark/src/chassis/feed.ts:18-25`. [verified]
- `f:mt7qcl` The scaffold's sitemap route builds its rows by hand from `site.all()` plus the site root, with `lastmod` taken from `date` alone, while `buildSitemapView` prefers `updated` over `date` and accepts `extraRoutes`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/routes/sitemap.xml/+server.ts:7-15`, `src/lib/delivery/views.ts:32`. [verified]
- `f:mtltw7` The delivery layer builds canonical URLs, `og:url`, and sitemap `loc` values as the configured origin concatenated with a leading-slash path, with no normalization, so the origin carries no trailing slash. Source: `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:147,170`, `src/lib/delivery/views.ts:25,31`. [verified]
- `f:n07oiu` `createSiteIndexes` takes per-concept globs of the form `import.meta.glob(pattern, { query: '?raw', import: 'default', eager: true })`; a lazy glob, or one without `?raw` and `import: 'default'`, hands the parser functions or module objects, and the index build throws `expected input to be a string or buffer`. Source: `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:83-85,100-101`, `src/lib/content/frontmatter.ts:392-398`, `templates/waymark/src/chassis/content.ts:9-23`. [verified]
- `f:n33lwb` The `config` (site config) argument of `createSiteIndexes` does not affect the built indexes; `buildSiteDescriptors` keeps the parameter for API stability and reads each concept's own routing instead. Source: `src/lib/delivery/site-descriptors.ts:8-16`. [verified]
- `f:nbn6j0` `buildExcerpt(body, { description?, maxChars? })` returns the trimmed frontmatter `description` when present, else the stripped body cut at a word boundary near `maxChars` (default 200) with an ellipsis, and the content index uses it for every entry's `excerpt`. Source: `src/lib/content/excerpt.ts#buildExcerpt`, `src/lib/delivery/content-index.ts:130`. [verified]
- `f:nibegw` `@glw907/cairn-cms/delivery/data` exports `buildSiteDescriptors`, `buildSitemapView`, `renderJsonLdScript`, and `buildExcerpt` as pure functions a site calls to hand-build concept descriptors, sitemap rows, a JSON-LD script tag, and excerpts. Source: `src/lib/delivery/data.ts:57-70`. [verified]
- `f:nnup2g` The scaffold's `publicRoutesConfig.defaultImage` is `siteMeta.origin + '/og/default.png'`, the social-card fallback for an entry with no resolved hero and no string `image`, but the scaffold ships no `static/` directory and no such file, so the fallback `og:image` 404s until the developer adds the file or changes `defaultImage`. Source: `templates/waymark/src/chassis/public-routes.ts:18`, `src/lib/delivery/public-routes.ts:144-156`. [verified]

### share-a-draft-preview

- `f:nssepj` `mintPreview(runtime, config, event, { concept, entryId })` and `revokePreview(runtime, event, { concept, entryId })` are callable from a site's own admin-guarded route; each resolves the session and checks the access map first, throwing (a 403, or a redirect to sign-in) on a session refusal, returns an `outcome` value for an unknown concept, a bad id, or (mint only) no draft, and logs the same `preview.token.*` record the built-in actions do. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/preview.ts:88-205`. [verified]

### act-on-newly-published-entries

- `f:ohq7b9` `editor.publishActions` on the adapter is a plain-data list of `{ label, href, concepts? }` next-step links that `editLoad` resolves server-side and shows beside the publish-success strip for the entry that just went live; `{concept}` and `{id}` in `href` are replaced with that entry's identity, `concepts` limits a link to the named concept ids, and the list is validated once when the content routes are constructed, so a blank label, a blank href, or an undeclared concept id throws at server start. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/publish-actions.ts#normalizePublishActions`, `src/lib/sveltekit/publish-actions.ts:38-77`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts:351-355`. [verified]
- `f:ozz6fs` Only `{concept}` and `{id}` are substituted in a `publishActions` href, verbatim and without URL encoding; any other brace token stays literal. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/publish-actions.ts#resolvePublishActions`, `src/lib/sveltekit/publish-actions.ts:74`. [verified]

### add-a-custom-admin-screen

- `f:pb0vh9` `AdminTable` takes `density` `'xs'` or `'sm'` (default `'sm'`) and `zebra`; `EmptyState` takes `headingLevel` `'p'` (default), `'h1'`, `'h2'`, or `'h3'`; `Pagination` shows a range line only when both `totalItems` and `pageSize` are given, and a page-size select only when a non-empty `pageSizeOptions` and `onPageSizeChange` are both given. Source: `src/lib/admin-toolkit/Pagination.svelte:26-57`, `src/lib/admin-toolkit/AdminTable.svelte:44`, `src/lib/admin-toolkit/EmptyState.svelte:32`. [verified]
- `f:ph6kjg` The `waitUntil` passed to `createD1AuditSink` is bound to its `ExecutionContext` (`ctx.waitUntil.bind(ctx)`), because the unbound method typechecks and then throws 'Illegal invocation' in workerd; the sink catches that, logs `audit.sink.write_failed` with reason `wait_until_failed`, and the insert runs unprotected, so the row can be lost. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/audit-sink.ts#createD1AuditSink`, `src/lib/sveltekit/audit-sink.ts:81-90`. [verified]
- `f:pmmtdv` `createSectionAction<Env, Db>` does not infer `Env` from an unannotated `resolveDb` parameter, so a site annotates that parameter or passes explicit type arguments; otherwise `Env` becomes `unknown` and binding reads off `env` stop typechecking. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/section-action.ts#createSectionAction`. [verified]
- `f:ppqu4v` `loadPreview` imports `$app/environment` dynamically inside a try/catch at call time, so bundling a `/sveltekit` export (such as `createD1AuditSink` for a Cron Worker) with plain esbuild outside Vite needs no `$app/environment` alias. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/preview.ts:414-439`. [verified]
- `f:pswc3n` `ExpandableRow` ignores a row click whose target sits inside an element carrying `data-cairn-inert-cell`, so an interactive summary cell wraps its content in that attribute instead of calling `stopPropagation()`; the trigger button needs no attribute. Source: `src/lib/admin-toolkit/ExpandableRow.svelte:23-28,104-111`. [verified]
- `f:pyfbqv` `MediaPicker` (from `/admin-toolkit`) takes `entries: MediaLibraryEntry[]`, the array `mediaLibraryLoad` returns as `MediaLibraryData.assets`, and calls `onselect` with a `MediaSelection` of `{ entry, ref, alt }`, where `ref` is the formatted `media:` token and `alt` is the manifest alt. Source: `src/lib/admin/MediaPicker.svelte:53-60,136-138`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-media-library.ts:37`. [verified]
- `f:q4jyat` The packaged admin sheet defines `cairn-text-warning` (color `--cairn-warning-ink`) and `cairn-text-success` (color `--color-positive-ink`), each with a light-theme literal fallback; the bracketed `text-[var(--cairn-warning-ink)]` and `text-[var(--color-positive-ink)]` forms are not compiled into that sheet. Source: `scripts/build/admin-css.input.css:12-22,127-132`. [verified]
- `f:qk0l7p` `ContentFormFailure` (from `/sveltekit`) is one flat interface with every field optional (`error`, `brokenLinks`, `body`, `inboundLinks`, `inboundKind`, `id`, `hash`, `usage`, `foundIn`) and types a content route's `form` prop for any content or media action's failure; the per-action failure shapes are module-internal. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-shared.ts:29-57`, `src/lib/sveltekit/index.ts:49`. [verified]
- `f:qkr057` `CairnAdminShell`'s `themeOverride` (`'cairn-admin'` or `'cairn-admin-dark'`) pins the admin theme, skips the theme-cookie and OS color-scheme checks, and removes the theme toggle. Source: `src/lib/admin/CairnAdminShell.svelte:55,310-335`. [verified]
- `f:qmhbgs` `EditPage`'s boolean `spellcheckOverride` pins spellcheck on or off over the stored preference and hides the spellcheck toggle; `false` opens an editor that never starts the spellcheck Worker or fetches its wasm and dictionary. Source: `src/lib/admin/EditPage.svelte:104-111,397-402`. [verified]
- `f:qz4gj2` The admin shell's favicon and sidebar brand mark are fixed to the cairn glyph and wordmark, and the only site identity it shows is `siteName` from the site config (in the page title and the topbar), with no adapter option or shell prop to replace either. Source: `src/lib/admin/CairnAdminShell.svelte:680-681,845,1063-1068`. [verified]

### arrange-the-admin-sidebar

- `f:rnwver` Declaring `editor.nav` (`configPath`, `menuName`, `label`, and an optional `maxDepth` defaulting to 2) on the adapter turns on the navigation editor at `/admin/nav` for `menus.<menuName>` in the site-config YAML at `configPath`, where a save is validated against `maxDepth` and head-guarded; without it `/admin/nav` answers 404, and a `navLayout` that references the `nav` screen throws at server start with `navLayout: screen "nav" is referenced but no navMenu is configured`. The public layout reads the same menu with `readMenu(siteConfig, name, maxDepth)`. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/nav-routes.ts:79-170`, `src/lib/sveltekit/admin-nav.ts:218-219`, `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts:345`. [verified]
- `f:rshs6k` The `attention` dep on `createCairnAdmin` or `createContentRoutes` is a function taking `{ editor, event }` that returns `AttentionItem[]` (`href`, `count`, and an optional `label` defaulting to 'pending items'); the engine awaits it once per admin shell load after nav resolution and `navFilter`, drops an item with a non-positive count, an href matching no nav entry visible to the session, or a duplicate href (first wins), and a throw fails the whole shell load, so a site that wants graceful degradation catches inside the callback and returns an empty array. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-shell.ts:193-205`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts:228-243`. [verified]
- `f:rx9v6d` `navFilter` receives the resolved `navLayout` top-level items (sections and loose entries) and `{ editor, event }`, runs fresh on every request after every built-in gate and before `attention`, never receives the fallback group of unreferenced engine screens (hide one with `hidden: true` in `navLayout`), and hides links only, so the route it hides still needs its own server-side gate. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts#ContentRoutesConfig.navFilter`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-shell.ts:189-192`. [verified]
- `f:rzfdqw` A menu node is `{ label, url?, children? }`, where a missing `url` makes a label-only group header; validation requires a non-empty label, caps a label at 500 characters, caps a url at 2048 and restricts it to the schemes `/`, `#`, `http(s)://`, `mailto:`, and `tel:`, caps the whole tree at 200 nodes and its depth at `maxDepth`, and drops any other key; `readMenu` returns an empty array for an absent menu and throws a `NavValidationError` (condition `config.site-config-invalid`) on any violation. Source: `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts#validateNavTree`, `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts:14-71,345-349`. [verified]
- `f:seum1o` A `navLayout` site entry icon, or an engine ref's icon override, is one of the 27 names in `ADMIN_NAV_ICON_NAMES` (listed in the sveltekit reference), and any other name throws a `navLayout:`-prefixed error at server start. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/admin-nav.ts#ADMIN_NAV_ICON_NAMES`. [verified]

### turn-on-tidy

- `f:shv6wv` The Settings (tidy conventions) save and the Tags (vocabulary) load and save read and commit the site-config file at `editor.nav.configPath` when the adapter declares `editor.nav`, and otherwise the fallback `src/lib/site.config.yaml`, so a site whose config lives elsewhere (the scaffold's is `src/theme/site.config.yaml`) keeps `editor.nav` declared or those saves answer 404 'Site config not found' and the Tags screen lists no vocabulary. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-settings.ts:123,198-199,297,417`. [verified]
- `f:sl0igk` An editor's nav, tidy-conventions, or vocabulary save rewrites only its own block of the site-config YAML and keeps every other key, comment, and key order, so a hand comment inside the replaced block is lost while comments elsewhere survive. Source: `src/lib/nav/site-config.ts#setMenu`. [verified]
- `f:tcadyk` `createCairnAdmin({ runtime, tidy })` forwards `tidy.client`, a factory taking `{ apiKey }` and returning a `TidyClient` whose `tidy(request, options)` resolves `{ corrected, refused, tokens: { input, output } }`, so a site can route tidy through its own gateway without Anthropic SDK types. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/cairn-admin.ts:43-46,113-119`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts:40-69,201`. [verified]
- `f:tcvrgk` The tidy action enforces its own 30-second deadline (`tidy.timeoutMs` overrides it), so a slow model call becomes a retryable `fail(502)` with 'Tidy could not finish. Try again.' and a `tidy.failed` record with `reason: 'timeout'` instead of a platform timeout. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-context.ts:202-208,274,372`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-tidy.ts:223-231`. [verified]

### restrict-admin-access

- `f:uhoyun` An href key in the access map matches by deepest path-segment prefix: `/admin/money` also governs `/admin/money/refunds` unless a deeper key is mapped, and `/admin/moneyx` never matches `/admin/money`; an href no key matches stays visible in the nav to any editor-capability session, while `requireAccess` and `createSectionAction` refuse it. Source: `src/lib/auth/access.ts:107-190`. [verified]
- `f:uotol3` A magic-link session resolves through a join on the `editor` row on every request, so a role change applies on the editor's next request with no new sign-in. Source: `src/lib/auth/store.ts#resolveSession`, `src/lib/auth/store.ts:219-232`. [verified]

### rotate-the-github-app-key

- `f:vg42j3` The GitHub installation token is cached per Worker isolate for 55 minutes, keyed by installation id, and minted only on a miss, so a warm isolate keeps using a token minted with an older key until its entry expires, and a cold isolate mints from the current `GITHUB_APP_PRIVATE_KEY_B64`. Source: `src/lib/github/signing.ts#createInstallationTokenCache`, `src/lib/github/signing.ts:105-121`, `src/lib/github/backend.ts:177`. [verified]

### debug-your-site

- `f:vg5zt8` `cairn health`'s `errors` check passes while the window holds at most `--error-threshold` of the engine's own error-level records (default 5) and fails above it, over a `--since` window that defaults to 24h. Source: `tool/cmd/cairn/health.go:18,23,57-58`, `tool/internal/health/check_errors.go:101-104`. [verified]
- `f:vi9xh0` `cairn help agents` prints the tool's whole contract for a program (exit codes and their precedence, stdout as payload and stderr as diagnostics, skip versus unknown, the schema URLs) from inside the binary, because `go install` installs no docs tree. Source: `tool/cmd/cairn/help_agents.go:5-20`, `tool/cmd/cairn/messages.go:201-254`. [verified]
- `f:vih1k1` `auth.session.destroyed` logs at sign-out only when the deleted session row was still live, carrying the row's `email`; a stale cookie or an already-expired row is deleted with no record, so an alert on it counts live sign-outs only. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/auth-routes.ts:424-429,459-468`. [verified]
- `f:vq634h` `auth.channel.session.destroyed` fires on three paths (the channel's logout, a confirm clearing the session row an incoming cookie named, and `resolveSubject` revoking a session the site's `verify` hook refused), only when the deleted row was still live, and carries only a `correlationId`, never the roster subject. Source: `src/lib/auth-channel/factory.ts:633-657,1041-1053`, `docs/reference/log-events.md:91`. [verified]
- `f:vq8cta` `commit.succeeded` and `commit.failed` carry `concept` for an entry commit and `scope` (`nav`, `settings`, `vocabulary`, or `media`) for a non-entry commit, never both, so a site concept named `media` or `nav` cannot be confused with those surfaces. Source: `src/lib/sveltekit/commit-log.ts:11-25`, `src/lib/sveltekit/nav-routes.ts:153`, `src/lib/sveltekit/content-routes-settings.ts:302,439`. [verified]
- `f:vs9g9e` When the build cannot derive the site facts from the adapter (the adapter throws for an unrelated reason), `checkSiteFacts` reports the committed `site-facts.json` as current rather than stale. Source: `src/lib/vite/internal.ts:445-468`. [verified]

### run-cairn-audit-on-your-site

- `f:vsbkl6` `cairn-audit --rendered` visits `/admin/posts`, `/admin/pages`, `/admin/vocabulary`, `/admin/media`, `/admin/editors`, and `/admin/login` unless `rendered.pages` replaces the list, and `rendered.extraPages` is appended to whichever list applies; a page that answers outside 2xx fails the run with exit 2, so a site whose concepts are not named `posts` and `pages` sets `rendered.pages`. Source: `src/lib/audit/config.ts:79-86,160-165,327-330`, `src/lib/audit/rendered.ts:205-214`, `src/lib/audit/bin.ts:74-76`. [verified]
- `f:wfv8qm` A static `cairn-audit` finding is suppressed by `cairn-audit-disable-next-line <rule-id> -- <reason>` in an HTML, `//`, or `/* */` comment; a directive with no rule, no reason, or nothing to silence raises an error-tier `suppression` finding that no directive can suppress. Source: `src/lib/audit/suppress.ts:19-31,209-224,265-282`. [verified]
- `f:x84fah` A `rendered.allowlist` entry needs `page`, `selector`, and `reason` and takes an optional `rule`; an entry that no longer matches becomes its own stale or dead finding at the named rule's tier, or at error tier when it names no rule, and an entry whose selector cannot parse is reported at advisory tier. Source: `src/lib/audit/config.ts:89-101,236-256`, `src/lib/audit/rendered/findings.ts:91-136,214-245`. [verified]
- `f:xj180v` A rendered `cairn-audit` run reads its session cookie from `CAIRN_AUDIT_COOKIES`; without one every admin route redirects to the sign-in card, and the run refuses with an error naming that variable and exits 2 rather than reporting a clean result. Source: `src/lib/audit/rendered.ts:126-135,162`, `src/lib/audit/bin.ts:72-76`. [verified]
- `f:xjp2mx` `cairn-audit.config.json`'s `static.sourceScope` (default `src`) names the directories the `log-event-grammar` and `log-secret-field` rules walk for `.ts` and `.svelte` files. Source: `src/lib/audit/config.ts:21,115-119,310`. [verified]
- `f:y1mjsk` `cairn-audit.config.json`'s `sheet` accepts one path or a list of compiled stylesheets the class checks resolve against, and a listed sheet that does not exist fails the run with an error naming it. Source: `src/lib/audit/config.ts:152-159,324-326`, `src/lib/audit/run.ts:155-173`. [verified]

## Harvest record

From the first half of the arm (pages add-a-custom-admin-screen through design-your-site):

Pages whose facts are entirely covered by another page: none in this slice; every page in the first 16 contributes at least one fact not stated elsewhere, though `define-an-adapter-and-schema.md` and `declare-your-own-concept.md` overlap heavily with `content-model.md` on the routing-shorthand enumeration (kept as cross-page duplicates rather than dropped, since each page states it as a load-bearing precondition for its own task).

Facts that read as decisions or opinions rather than facts (not harvested as standalone facts, only noted):
- `choose-an-ai-posture.md`'s "What declining actually buys you, honestly" section is an editorial/honesty framing about robots.txt's non-enforcement nature, not a discrete verifiable fact (the underlying claim, that robots.txt has no blocking mechanism, is general web knowledge, not a cairn fact).
- `architecture.md`'s framing that the three-way core/adapter/SvelteKit split is "deliberate" is a design rationale, not a fact an implementer acts on directly (the resulting file-placement rule is captured as a fact instead).
- `auth-channel-security-model.md`'s narrative about three earlier design rounds each failing by violating the identity-keyed-denial rule is historical rationale, not an actionable fact; the resulting rule itself is harvested.
- `design-your-site.md`'s framing that "the chassis is deliberately generous, not minimal" is a design philosophy statement; the actionable consequence (check `src/chassis/README.md`'s dependents table before removing a piece) is harvested instead.
- `build-a-site-by-hand.md`'s milestone narrative ("this proves the deploy loop before you have any content worth deploying") is pedagogical framing, not a fact.

From the second half of the arm (pages enable-tidy through wire-the-delivery-surface):

- Pages whose facts are entirely covered by another page: none outright, but `rotate-the-github-app-key.md`'s procedural steps are almost entirely an operational runbook restating GitHub's own key-rotation mechanics (cited and linked), with only the base64-encoding command and the retired doctor bin's Worker-secret integration being cairn-specific; a future harvest could treat the GitHub-platform half as background rather than a fact set.
- `upgrade-cairn.md` is thin on its own: almost every fact is either a cross-reference to `migration-notes.md` (already harvested above) or a generic "run the doctor, typecheck, test" procedure with no cairn-internal mechanism of its own to verify.
- Facts that read as decisions or opinions rather than verifiable facts (listed, not harvested as facts):
  - "Truly excellent documentation matters more than perfectly compliant documentation" (docs-register.md) is a stated editorial value, not a fact about cairn.
  - security-model.md's framing that the realistic threat is "a compromised editor account... not an anonymous public attacker" is a threat-model judgment call, restated identically in render-safety.md; it shapes the design but is not itself falsifiable against code.
  - sign-in-through-your-organization.md's advice to "Set it to hours, not the maximum" for the Access session duration is a recommendation, not an enforced constraint (identity mode has no code-level ceiling on the operator-set duration).
  - enable-tidy.md's claim that Sonnet 5 is "the judgment floor for a light copy-edit" is an editorial rationale for the default choice, not a testable fact.
  - wire-the-delivery-surface.md's own content-type investigation explicitly flags itself as "measured locally... not verified against a live deployed zone" and generalizes to no other extension; treat its narrow, dated, self-scoped conclusion as evidence rather than a settled cross-version fact.

- Facts-container HEAD repair 2026-09-22: 8 bullets sourced to the now-removed legacy JS doctor
  package deleted (the `github.app` save-chain bullet in add-cairn-to-a-sveltekit-app.md, the
  `config.csrf-disable-missing` both-files bullet in build-a-site-by-hand.md, the
  `config.tidy-key-missing` `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`-probing bullet in enable-tidy.md, the
  rotation-order runbook and its two `github.app` bullets in rotate-the-github-app-key.md, and the
  the retired doctor bin's `--from`/`--repo` upgrade step and its typecheck-scope bullet in
  upgrade-cairn.md), since the claims describe the removed JS CLI's implementation details
  (module comments, function names, specific flags) with no engine-side (non-`tool/`) source left
  to carry them.

## Provenance

Harvested 2026-09-15 from docs/extend/*, split into two slices (pages 1-16, pages 17-32) that
merge into this one file. Both slices tightened 2026-09-15 (one net addition in the second half:
the navLayout screen-id-universe resolution fact added alongside the retagged original claim).
The one remaining candidate (migrate-existing-content.md's inline-validation workflow claim) is
carried in `docs/internal/docs-friction-log.md`'s open findings; the one rejected fact
(enable-tidy.md's dev-backend Anthropic-client-stub claim) is carried in its own page section, not
re-litigated here.
